(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types obj)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - obj)
    (cats ?x - obj)
    (sneeze ?x - obj)
    (texture ?x - obj)
    (spring ?x - obj)
    (stupendous ?x - obj)
    (collect ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (next ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (vase ?x - obj ?y - obj)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?o0 - obj ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?o0)
      (cats ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (not (vase ?o0 ?o1))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?o0 - obj ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)

      (hand ?o0)

      (texture ?o2)
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o2))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?o0 - obj ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?o0)
      (sneeze ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o2))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?o0 - obj ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj ?o3 - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?o0)
      (texture ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (stupendous ?o3)
      (next ?o0 ?o1)
      (collect ?o1 ?o3)
      (collect ?o2 ?o3)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o1))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?o0 - obj ?o1 - obj ?o2 - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?o0)
      (spring ?o1)
      (spring ?o2)
      (next ?o0 ?o1)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o1))
    )

      (sneeze ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)

      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
    )

<domain>
(define (domain jackofalltrades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types obj)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - obj)
    (cats ?x - obj)
    (texture ?x - obj)
    (sneeze ?x - obj)
    (spring ?x - obj)
    (stupendous ?x - obj)
    (next ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (vase ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (collect ?x - obj ?y - obj)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj ?u - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (stupendous ?u)
      (next ?x ?y)
      (collect ?y ?u)
      (collect ?z ?u)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?x)
      (spring ?y)
      (spring ?z)
      (next ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )
)

    (texture object_5)
    (texture object_6)

================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain jackofalltrades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types obj)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - obj)
    (cats ?x - obj)
    (texture ?x - obj)
    (sneeze ?x - obj)
    (spring ?x - obj)
    (stupendous ?x - obj)
    (next ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (vase ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (collect ?x - obj ?y - obj)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj ?u - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (stupendous ?u)
      (next ?x ?y)
      (collect ?y ?u)
      (collect ?z ?u)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?x)
      (spring ?y)
      (spring ?z)
      (next ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj ?z - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )
)

(define (domain jackofalltrades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types thing)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - thing)
    (cats ?x - thing)
    (texture ?x - thing)
    (sneeze ?x - thing)
    (spring ?x - thing)
    (stupendous ?x - thing)
    (next ?x - thing ?y - thing)
    (vase ?x - thing ?y - thing)
    (collect ?x - thing ?y - thing)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?h - thing ?c - thing ?t - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (next ?c ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?c))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?h - thing ?c - thing ?t - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
      (next ?c ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?h - thing ?s - thing ?t - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?s)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?s - thing ?t1 - thing ?t2 - thing ?u - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t1)
      (texture ?t2)
      (stupendous ?u)
      (next ?s ?t1)
      (collect ?t1 ?u)
      (collect ?t2 ?u)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?s ?t2)
      (not (next ?s ?t1))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?c - thing ?s1 - thing ?s2 - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?c)
      (spring ?s1)
      (spring ?s2)
      (next ?c ?s1)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?c ?s2)
      (not (next ?c ?s1))
    )

Once paltry is performed the following facts will be false: vase object_0 object_1
To perform sip action, the following facts need to be true: hand object_0, cats object_1, texture object_2, next object_0 object_2, and next object_1 object_2

<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - object)
    (cats ?x - object)
    (sneeze ?x - object)
    (spring ?x - object)
    (stupendous ?x - object)
    (texture ?x - object)
    (collect ?x - object ?y - object)
    (next ?x - object ?y - object)
    (vase ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object ?w - object)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (stupendous ?w)
      (next ?x ?y)
      (collect ?y ?w)
      (collect ?z ?w)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?x)
      (spring ?y)
      (spring ?z)
      (next ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )
)
</domain>

Here is the human-readable action sequence that follows the given plan exactly.

1. At time 1, perform **memory** with **object_1, object_6, object_7**.  
   - Since **object_1** is a cat, **object_6** and **object_7** are springs, and **object_1** is currently connected to **object_6**, switch that connection so that **object_1** is now connected to **object_7** instead.

2. At time 2, perform **sip** with **object_9, object_1, object_7**.  
   - Since **object_9** is a hand, **object_1** is a cat, **object_7** has texture, and both **object_9** and **object_1** are connected to **object_7**, create a **vase** relation between **object_9** and **object_1**, and remove the connection from **object_9** to **object_7**.

3. At time 3, perform **memory** with **object_1, object_7, object_6**.  
   - Since **object_1** is a cat, **object_7** and **object_6** are springs, and **object_1** is currently connected to **object_7**, switch that connection back so that **object_1** is now connected to **object_6**.

4. At time 4, perform **paltry** with **object_9, object_1, object_6**.  
   - Since **object_9** is a hand, **object_1** is a cat, **object_6** has texture, **object_9** is in the **vase** relation with **object_1**, and **object_1** is connected to **object_6**, create a connection from **object_9** to **object_6** and remove the **vase** relation between **object_9** and **object_1**.

Final result:
- **object_8** is still connected to **object_7**
- **object_9** is now connected to **object_6**

So the goal is satisfied.

<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (next ?x - object ?y - object)
    (vase ?x - object ?y - object)
    (hand ?x - object)
    (cats ?x - object)
    (sneeze ?x - object)
    (texture ?x - object)
    (spring ?x - object)
    (stupendous ?x - object)
    (collect ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object ?w - object)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (stupendous ?w)
      (next ?x ?y)
      (collect ?y ?w)
      (collect ?z ?w)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?x)
      (spring ?y)
      (spring ?z)
      (next ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )
)
</domain>

(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (next ?x - object ?y - object)
    (vase ?x - object ?y - object)
    (hand ?x - object)
    (cats ?x - object)
    (sneeze ?x - object)
    (texture ?x - object)
    (spring ?x - object)
    (stupendous ?x - object)
    (collect ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (cats ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (next ?x ?z)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?x ?y)
      (not (next ?x ?z))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object ?w - object)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?x)
      (texture ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (stupendous ?w)
      (next ?x ?y)
      (collect ?y ?w)
      (collect ?z ?w)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?x)
      (spring ?y)
      (spring ?z)
      (next ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (next ?x ?y))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object ?z - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?x)
      (sneeze ?y)
      (texture ?z)
      (next ?y ?z)
      (vase ?x ?y)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?x ?z)
      (not (vase ?x ?y))
    )
  )
)

   Since `object_9` is a hand object, `object_3` is a sneeze object, `object_5` is a texture object, `next object_3 object_5` holds, and `vase object_9 object_3` holds, create **`next object_9 object_5`** and remove **`vase object_9 object_3`**.

5. **At time 5, perform `sip` with `object_9`, `object_0`, and `object_5`.**  
   Since `object_9` is a hand object, `object_0` is a cats object, `object_5` is a texture object, and both `next object_9 object_5` and `next object_0 object_5` hold, create **`vase object_9 object_0`** and remove **`next object_9 object_5`**.

6. **At time 6, perform `memory` with `object_0`, `object_5`, and `object_7`.**  
   Since `object_0` is a cats object, `object_5` and `object_7` are spring objects, and `next object_0 object_5` holds, change the link from `object_0 -> object_5` to **`object_0 -> object_7`**.

7. **At time 7, perform `paltry` with `object_9`, `object_0`, and `object_7`.**  
   Since `object_9` is a hand object, `object_0` is a cats object, `object_7` is a texture object, `vase object_9 object_0` holds, and `next object_0 object_7` holds, create **`next object_9 object_7`** and remove **`vase object_9 object_0`**.

**Result:** the goal **`next object_9 object_7`** is achieved.

(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - object)
    (cats ?x - object)
    (sneeze ?x - object)
    (texture ?x - object)
    (spring ?x - object)
    (stupendous ?x - object)
    (collect ?x - object ?y - object)
    (next ?x - object ?y - object)
    (vase ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?o0)
      (cats ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (not (vase ?o0 ?o1))
    )
  )

  (:action sip

    :precondition (and
      (hand ?o0)
      (cats ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o2))
    )
  )

  (:action clip

    :precondition (and
      (hand ?o0)
      (sneeze ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o2))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched

      (texture ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)

      (not (next ?o0 ?o1))
    )
  )

      (spring ?o2)

    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o1))
    )

<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types
    obj
    hand_t cats_t sneeze_t texture_t spring_t stupendous_t - obj
  )

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - obj)
    (cats ?x - obj)
    (sneeze ?x - obj)
    (texture ?x - obj)
    (spring ?x - obj)
    (stupendous ?x - obj)
    (collect ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (next ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (vase ?x - obj ?y - obj)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?o0 - hand_t ?o1 - cats_t ?o2 - texture_t)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?o0)
      (cats ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (not (vase ?o0 ?o1))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?o0 - hand_t ?o1 - cats_t ?o2 - texture_t)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?o0)
      (cats ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o2))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?o0 - hand_t ?o1 - sneeze_t ?o2 - texture_t)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?o0)
      (sneeze ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o2))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?o0 - sneeze_t ?o1 - texture_t ?o2 - texture_t ?o3 - stupendous_t)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?o0)
      (texture ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (stupendous ?o3)
      (next ?o0 ?o1)
      (collect ?o1 ?o3)
      (collect ?o2 ?o3)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o1))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?o0 - cats_t ?o1 - spring_t ?o2 - spring_t)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?o0)
      (spring ?o1)
      (spring ?o2)
      (next ?o0 ?o1)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o1))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?o0 - hand_t ?o1 - sneeze_t ?o2 - texture_t)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?o0)
      (sneeze ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (not (vase ?o0 ?o1))
    )
  )
)

      (not (next ?o0 ?o2))
    )
  )

    )
  )

   - Since `object_3` is a sneeze object, `object_5` and `object_6` are texture objects, `object_1` is stupendous, `object_3` is next to `object_5`, and both `object_5` and `object_6` are collected by `object_1`, redirect `object_3` from `object_5` to `object_6`.

6. **Time 5:** Perform **clip** with `object_11`, `object_3`, and `object_6`.  
   - Since `object_11` is a hand object, `object_3` is a sneeze object, `object_6` is a texture object, and both `object_3` and `object_11` are next to `object_6`, create `vase object_11 object_3` and remove `next object_11 object_6`.

7. **Time 6:** Perform **wretched** with `object_3`, `object_6`, `object_5`, and `object_1`.  
   - Since `object_3` is now next to `object_6`, and both `object_6` and `object_5` are collected by `object_1`, redirect `object_3` from `object_6` back to `object_5`.

8. **Time 7:** Perform **tightfisted** with `object_11`, `object_3`, and `object_5`.  
   - Since `object_11` is holding the vase relation with `object_3`, and `object_3` is next to `object_5`, make `object_11` next to `object_5` and remove the vase relation between `object_11` and `object_3`.

9. **Time 8:** Perform **sip** with `object_9`, `object_0`, and `object_5`.  
   - Since `object_9` is a hand object, `object_0` is a cats object, `object_5` is a texture object, and both `object_9` and `object_0` are next to `object_5`, create `vase object_9 object_0` and remove `next object_9 object_5`.

10. **Time 9:** Perform **sip** with `object_11`, `object_0`, and `object_5`.  
    - Since `object_11` is a hand object, `object_0` is a cats object, `object_5` is a texture object, and both `object_11` and `object_0` are next to `object_5`, create `vase object_11 object_0` and remove `next object_11 object_5`.

11. **Time 10:** Perform **memory** with `object_0`, `object_5`, and `object_7`.  
    - Since `object_0` is a cats object, `object_5` and `object_7` are spring objects, and `object_0` is next to `object_5`, redirect `object_0` from `object_5` to `object_7`.

12. **Time 11:** Perform **paltry** with `object_9`, `object_0`, and `object_7`.  
    - Since `object_9` is a hand object, `object_0` is a cats object, `object_7` is a texture object, `object_9` has a vase relation with `object_0`, and `object_0` is next to `object_7`, make `object_9` next to `object_7` and remove the vase relation between `object_9` and `object_0`.

13. **Time 12:** Perform **paltry** with `object_11`, `object_0`, and `object_7`.  
    - Since `object_11` is a hand object, `object_0` is a cats object, `object_7` is a texture object, `object_11` has a vase relation with `object_0`, and `object_0` is next to `object_7`, make `object_11` next to `object_7` and remove the vase relation between `object_11` and `object_0`.

**Final goal achieved:**  
- `object_10` is next to `object_7`  
- `object_11` is next to `object_7`  
- `object_9` is next to `object_7`

<domain>
(define (domain jackofalltrades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - object)
    (cats ?x - object)
    (sneeze ?x - object)
    (texture ?x - object)
    (spring ?x - object)
    (stupendous ?x - object)
    (collect ?x - object ?y - object)
    (next ?x - object ?y - object)
    (vase ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?o0)
      (cats ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (not (vase ?o0 ?o1))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?o0)
      (cats ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o2))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?o0)
      (sneeze ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o2))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object ?o3 - object)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?o0)
      (texture ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (stupendous ?o3)
      (next ?o0 ?o1)
      (collect ?o1 ?o3)
      (collect ?o2 ?o3)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o1))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?o0)
      (spring ?o1)
      (spring ?o2)
      (next ?o0 ?o1)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (not (next ?o0 ?o1))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?o0 - object ?o1 - object ?o2 - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?o0)
      (sneeze ?o1)
      (texture ?o2)
      (next ?o1 ?o2)
      (vase ?o0 ?o1)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?o0 ?o2)
      (not (vase ?o0 ?o1))
    )
  )
)
</domain>

<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - object)
    (cats ?x - object)
    (sneeze ?x - object)
    (texture ?x - object)
    (spring ?x - object)
    (stupendous ?x - object)
    (collect ?t - object ?s - object)
    (next ?x - object ?y - object)
    (vase ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?h - object ?c - object ?t - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (next ?c ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?c))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?h - object ?c - object ?t - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
      (next ?c ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?h - object ?s - object ?t - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?s)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?s - object ?t1 - object ?t2 - object ?st - object)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t1)
      (texture ?t2)
      (stupendous ?st)
      (next ?s ?t1)
      (collect ?t1 ?st)
      (collect ?t2 ?st)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?s ?t2)
      (not (next ?s ?t1))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?c - object ?sp1 - object ?sp2 - object)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?c)
      (spring ?sp1)
      (spring ?sp2)
      (next ?c ?sp1)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?c ?sp2)
      (not (next ?c ?sp1))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?h - object ?s - object ?t - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (vase ?h ?s)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?s))
    )
  )
)

<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - object)
    (cats ?x - object)
    (sneeze ?x - object)
    (texture ?x - object)
    (spring ?x - object)
    (stupendous ?x - object)
    (collect ?x - object ?y - object)
    (next ?x - object ?y - object)
    (vase ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?h - object ?c - object ?t - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (next ?c ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?c))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?h - object ?c - object ?t - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
      (next ?c ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?h - object ?s - object ?t - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?s)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?s - object ?from_t - object ?to_t - object ?u - object)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?from_t)
      (texture ?to_t)
      (stupendous ?u)
      (next ?s ?from_t)
      (collect ?from_t ?u)
      (collect ?to_t ?u)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?s ?to_t)
      (not (next ?s ?from_t))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?c - object ?from_s - object ?to_s - object)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?c)
      (spring ?from_s)
      (spring ?to_s)
      (next ?c ?from_s)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?c ?to_s)
      (not (next ?c ?from_s))
    )
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?h - object ?s - object ?t - object)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (vase ?h ?s)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?s))
    )
  )
)
</domain>

(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)

  (:types
    object
    hand_obj cat_obj sneeze_obj texture_obj spring_obj stupendous_obj - object
  )

  (:predicates
    (hand ?x - object)
    (cats ?x - object)
    (sneeze ?x - object)
    (texture ?x - object)
    (spring ?x - object)
    (stupendous ?x - object)
    (collect ?x - object ?y - object)
    (next ?x - object ?y - object)
    (vase ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?h - hand_obj ?c - cat_obj ?t - texture_obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (next ?c ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?h ?t)
      (not (vase ?h ?c))
    )
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?h - hand_obj ?c - cat_obj ?t - texture_obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (cats ?c)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
      (next ?c ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?c)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?h - hand_obj ?s - sneeze_obj ?t - texture_obj)
    :precondition (and
      (hand ?h)
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?t)
      (next ?s ?t)
      (next ?h ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (vase ?h ?s)
      (not (next ?h ?t))
    )
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?s - sneeze_obj ?from_t - texture_obj ?to_t - texture_obj ?k - stupendous_obj)
    :precondition (and
      (sneeze ?s)
      (texture ?from_t)
      (texture ?to_t)
      (stupendous ?k)
      (next ?s ?from_t)
      (collect ?from_t ?k)
      (collect ?to_t ?k)
    )
    :effect (and
      (next ?s ?to_t)
      (not (next ?s ?from_t))
    )
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?c - cat_obj ?from_sp - spring_obj ?to_sp - spring_obj)
    :precondition (and
      (cats ?c)
      (spring ?from_sp)
      (spring ?to_sp)
      (next ?c ?from_sp)
    )
    :effect (and

      (not (next ?c ?from_sp))
